# Windows

Every project across all 39 lists that runs natively on Windows, or is reachable through WSL2 — **10,687** of 11,762 distinct repos, deduplicated to one row per repo.

Native first, then WSL2-only, each block by stars. A Homebrew-only project that ships a Windows build shows its releases page instead of a command that cannot run here.

[← back to the mega list](../README.md) · [7,460 inferred →](windows-inferred.md)

Page **2** of 5, because this list is longer than the 512 KB GitHub will render in one file. In order: [1](windows.md) · **2** · [3](windows-3.md) · [4](windows-4.md) · [5](windows-5.md) — [continue on page 3 →](windows-3.md)
